BabelStone Fonts

Test copy of the upstream BSH_IVS.html, rendered with the @free-fonts/babelstone-han@1.1.0 chunked webfonts from jsDelivr (babelstone-han.css) instead of locally installed fonts; it needs nothing else and works from any computer with Internet access. Every glyph in the tables should show the variant for its variation selector. A generated Standardized Variation Sequences section (1,059 more sequences) at the end makes the page cover all 3,952 variation sequences in the fonts. 24 IVD markers (*) missing from the upstream HTML table were restored from BSH_IVS.TXT of the same release. 本頁為上游 BSH_IVS.html 的測試副本，改用本包 1.1.0 的切片網頁字型（經 jsDelivr 載入）渲染，無其他依賴，任何聯網電腦皆可正確顯示；文末另附自動生成的標準化變體序列一節，合計涵蓋字型支援的全部 3,952 組異體字序列；上游 HTML 漏標的 24 個 IVD 已註冊標記（虛線底線）依同版本 BSH_IVS.TXT 補上。

BabelStone Han Variation Sequences

BabelStone Han v. 18.0.1 supports an experimental set of 2,893 ideographic variation sequences for the 1,407 CJK unified ideographs listed in the table below. 1,343 characters have two variants, 55 characters have three variants, seven characters have four variants, one character has five variants, and one character has nine variants. For each character, the first variant is always the same as the BabelStone Han glyph for the unmodified character. A plain text listing of the IVS sequences is available as BSH_IVS.TXT.

These ideographic variation sequences are not registered in the Ideographic Variation Database (IVD), and may be subject to change in future versions of the font. It is intended to register a BabelStone Collection in the IVD at a future date when the sequences listed here are stable.

The 384 variation selectors marked with an asterisk (e.g. "VS17*") indicate that the corresponding variation sequence is already registered in the IVD, and I think that the BabelStone glyph is an acceptable match for the glyph form for the registered IVS. All other variation sequences listed below should not be currently registered in the IVD (as of 2025-07-14). NB I plan to reassign most of the IVSes marked with an asterisk so they have a unique IVS sequence.

In principle, BabelStone Han follows the G-source (China) glyph forms, but in some cases it seems preferable to not follow the G-source glyph where it does not match expected glyph conventions for China, or where G-source glyph forms are inconsistent. In these cases, an IVS is assigned for the G-source glyph form, indicated by a double asterisk (**) after the source reference. There are currently 49 cases, but this number will be reduced when and if China makes glyph corrections for these characters. NB China consistently has an unjoined right leg of 鬼 in characters in the URO and Ext. A, but consistently joins the right leg of 鬼 in characters in Ext. B and later — for consistency, BabelStone Han has an unjoined right leg in all 鬼 characters, but currently does not provide an IVS for the alternative version because there are so many characters. China also has an inconsistent mix of characters with joined and unjoined right leg of 旡 — for consistency, BabelStone Han has an unjoined right leg when 旡 occurs in a lateral position, but a joined leg elsewhere.

Standardized Variation Sequences

In addition to the 2,893 ideographic variation sequences above, BabelStone Han supports 1,059 variation sequences with the standard variation selectors VS1–VS3 (U+FE00–FE02), listed below, so that this page covers every variation sequence in the fonts. This section is not part of the upstream chart: it is generated from the fonts' cmap format 14 tables and StandardizedVariants-18.0.0.txt.

本節不屬於上游對照表：由字型的 cmap format 14 表與 Unicode 標準化變體列表自動生成，列出 VS1–VS3 的 1,059 組序列，連同上方 2,893 組 IVS，涵蓋字型支援的全部異體字序列。

CJK Compatibility Ideographs (1,002)

Unicode normalization turns each CJK compatibility ideograph into its unified ideograph, losing its distinct glyph; the standardized variation sequence (unified ideograph + VS) listed here keeps that glyph. The Glyph and Compatibility Ideograph columns should therefore look the same. BabelStone Han supports 1,002 of the 1,002 such sequences defined in Unicode 18.0.0.

Code Point VS Plain With VS Description
U+0030 VS1 0 0︀ short diagonal stroke form — DIGIT ZERO
U+2018 VS1 ‘ ‘︀ non-fullwidth form — LEFT SINGLE QUOTATION MARK. Same glyph as the plain character (already its default form).

U+2019 VS1 ’ ’︀ non-fullwidth form — RIGHT SINGLE QUOTATION MARK. Same glyph as the plain character (already its default form).
U+2019 VS2 ’ ’︁ left-justified fullwidth form — RIGHT SINGLE QUOTATION MARK. Same glyph as the plain character (already its default form).

U+201D VS2 ” ”︁ left-justified fullwidth form — RIGHT DOUBLE QUOTATION MARK. Same glyph as the plain character (already its default form).
